# S · part 2

1,327 plugins, sdlc-model-council to sfeed. [Back to the index](../README.md)

- **sdlc-model-council**: Cross-model council — assess the models reachable via locally installed peer agentic CLIs (codex, agy, opencode) against a standardized problem stack, cast them into a roster, and run cross-model fan-out plays (v1: Diff+Synthesis) chosen per project. Built on a unified delegation contract with graded permission postures; in-session and uncontainerised, distinct from sdlc-workflows' Archon/Docker/DAG delegation ([SteveGJones/ai-first-sdlc-practices](https://github.com/SteveGJones/ai-first-sdlc-practices/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-model-council))
